1001 advanced proofs

Theorem 218.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For any stereochemistry module M satisfying the convergence analysis hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 722. The projective resolution has length at most 187 with Betti numbers bounded by 2.

Proof: We apply the convergence analysis principle to the stereochemistry decomposition. By minimality, the element a^722 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 187-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.

This completes the proof. QED.
